| 5 | [2 The Curse of Dimensionality for Classical Non-Parametric Models](https://www.semanticscholar.org/paper/9bfcf812b249d570823c6a0dff4a4781a40524d1)<br><sub>Yoshua Bengio, Olivier Delalleau, Nicolas Le Roux</sub><br><sub>A series of theoretical arguments support the claim that a large class of modern learning algorithms based on local kernels are sensitive to the curse of dimensionality, including local manifold learning algorithms such as Isomap and LLE, support vector classifiers with Gaussian or other local kernels, and graph-based semisupervised learning algorithms using a local similarity function.</sub> | - | - | 0 | 0 |
